Converting to string\n    2. Replacing comma with period (decimal separator)\n    3. Converting scientific notation to standard form\n    4. Converting to float\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Convert to string and strip whitespace\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    # Convert to float (handles scientific notation automatically)\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef detect_and_convert_unit(value, feature_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect if a value is using an alternative unit based on physiological range\n    and convert it to US conventional units if necessary.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or feature_name not in LAB_FEATURES_INFO:\n        return value\n\n    info = LAB_FEATURES_INFO[feature_name]\n    min_val = info['min']\n    max_val = info['max']\n    alt_factor = info.get('alt_factor')\n\n    # If the value is already within the expected range, no conversion needed\n    if min_val <= value <= max_val:\n        return value\n\n    # If there's no alternative unit conversion factor, keep as is\n    if alt_factor is None:\n        return value\n\n    # Try converting from alternative unit\n    converted_value = value * alt_factor\n\n    # If converted value falls within expected range, use it\n    if min_val <= converted_value <= max_val:\n        return converted_value\n\n    # If still outside range, return original (may be outlier)\n    return value\n\n\ndef harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize clinical lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading data from {input_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file)\n\n    print(f\"Initial dataset shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Initial row count: {len(df)}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Convert units to US conventional units\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Converting mixed units to US conventional units...\")\n    conversion_counts = {}\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature == 'patient_id':\n            continue\n\n        if feature in LAB_FEATURES_INFO:\n            # Apply unit detection and conversion\n            original_values = df_clean[feature].copy()\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].apply(\n                lambda x: detect_and_convert_unit(x, feature)\n            )\n\n            # Count how many values were converted\n            changed = (original_values != df_clean[feature]).sum()\n            if changed > 0:\n                conversion_counts[feature] = changed\n\n    if conversion_counts:\n        print(f\"Converted values in {len(conversion_counts)} features:\")\n        for feature, count in sorted(conversion_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:10]:\n            print(f\"  {feature}: {count} values converted\")\n\n    # Step 4: Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].round(2)\n\n    # Step 5: Format all numeric values to ensure X.XX format\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Formatting numeric values to X.XX format...\")\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else x)\n\n    # Save harmonized data\n    print(f\"\\nSaving harmonized data to {output_file}...\")\n    df_clean.to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n\n    print(f\"\\nHarmonization complete!\")\n    print(f\"Final dataset shape: {df_clean.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Output saved to: {output_file}\")\n\n    # Print some statistics\n    print(\"\\n=== Summary Statistics ===\")\n    print(f\"Input records: {initial_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Records with missing values removed: {removed_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Final complete records: {len(df_clean)}\")\n    print(f\"Data completeness: {len(df_clean)/initial_rows*100:.2f}%\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    input_file = \"/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv\"\n    output_file = \"/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv\"\n\n    harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":506,"cache_read_input_tokens":38067,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":506,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"04781ca8-62ae-41e6-866f-0d17a4601ca4","uuid":"d3cce918-bef0-4312-ab28-757fa8d74645"}

Converting to string\n    2. Replacing comma with period (decimal separator)\n    3. Converting scientific notation to standard form\n    4. Converting to float\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Convert to string and strip whitespace\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    # Convert to float (handles scientific notation automatically)\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef detect_and_convert_unit(value, feature_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect if a value is using an alternative unit based on physiological range\n    and convert it to US conventional units if necessary.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or feature_name not in LAB_FEATURES_INFO:\n        return value\n\n    info = LAB_FEATURES_INFO[feature_name]\n    min_val = info['min']\n    max_val = info['max']\n    alt_factor = info.get('alt_factor')\n\n    # If the value is already within the expected range, no conversion needed\n    if min_val <= value <= max_val:\n        return value\n\n    # If there's no alternative unit conversion factor, keep as is\n    if alt_factor is None:\n        return value\n\n    # Try converting from alternative unit\n    converted_value = value * alt_factor\n\n    # If converted value falls within expected range, use it\n    if min_val <= converted_value <= max_val:\n        return converted_value\n\n    # If still outside range, return original (may be outlier)\n    return value\n\n\ndef harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize clinical lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading data from {input_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file)\n\n    print(f\"Initial dataset shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Initial row count: {len(df)}\")\n\n    # Step 1: Clean all numeric values (handle scientific notation and comma decimals)\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Cleaning numeric values (scientific notation, comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in df.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(clean_numeric_value)\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with missing values\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    initial_rows = len(df)\n    df_clean = df.dropna()\n    removed_rows = initial_rows - len(df_clean)\n    print(f\"Removed {removed_rows} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {len(df_clean)}\")\n\n    if len(df_clean) == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No complete records remaining after removing missing values!\")\n        return\n\n    # Step 3: Convert units to US conventional units\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Converting mixed units to US conventional units...\")\n    conversion_counts = {}\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature == 'patient_id':\n            continue\n\n        if feature in LAB_FEATURES_INFO:\n            # Apply unit detection and conversion\n            original_values = df_clean[feature].copy()\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].apply(\n                lambda x: detect_and_convert_unit(x, feature)\n            )\n\n            # Count how many values were converted\n            changed = (original_values != df_clean[feature]).sum()\n            if changed > 0:\n                conversion_counts[feature] = changed\n\n    if conversion_counts:\n        print(f\"Converted values in {len(conversion_counts)} features:\")\n        for feature, count in sorted(conversion_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:10]:\n            print(f\"  {feature}: {count} values converted\")\n\n    # Step 4: Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].round(2)\n\n    # Step 5: Format all numeric values to ensure X.XX format\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Formatting numeric values to X.XX format...\")\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else x)\n\n    # Save harmonized data\n    print(f\"\\nSaving harmonized data to {output_file}...\")\n    df_clean.to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n\n    print(f\"\\nHarmonization complete!\")\n    print(f\"Final dataset shape: {df_clean.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Output saved to: {output_file}\")\n\n    # Print some statistics\n    print(\"\\n=== Summary Statistics ===\")\n    print(f\"Input records: {initial_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Records with missing values removed: {removed_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Final complete records: {len(df_clean)}\")\n    print(f\"Data completeness: {len(df_clean)/initial_rows*100:.2f}%\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    input_file = \"/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv\"\n    output_file = \"/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv\"\n\n    harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Converting to string\n    2. Replacing comma with period (decimal separator)\n    3. Converting scientific notation to standard form\n    4. Converting to float\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Convert to string and strip whitespace\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    # Convert to float (handles scientific notation automatically)\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef detect_and_convert_unit(value, feature_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect if a value is using an alternative unit based on physiological range\n    and convert it to US conventional units if necessary.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or feature_name not in LAB_FEATURES_INFO:\n        return value\n\n    info = LAB_FEATURES_INFO[feature_name]\n    min_val = info['min']\n    max_val = info['max']\n    alt_factor = info.get('alt_factor')\n\n    # If the value is already within the expected range, no conversion needed\n    if min_val <= value <= max_val:\n        return value\n\n    # If there's no alternative unit conversion factor, keep as is\n    if alt_factor is None:\n        return value\n\n    # Try converting from alternative unit\n    converted_value = value * alt_factor\n\n    # If converted value falls within expected range, use it\n    if min_val <= converted_value <= max_val:\n        return converted_value\n\n    # If still outside range, return original (may be outlier)\n    return value\n\n\ndef harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize clinical lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading data from {input_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file)\n\n    print(f\"Initial dataset shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Initial row count: {len(df)}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Convert units to US conventional units\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Converting mixed units to US conventional units...\")\n    conversion_counts = {}\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature == 'patient_id':\n            continue\n\n        if feature in LAB_FEATURES_INFO:\n            # Apply unit detection and conversion\n            original_values = df_clean[feature].copy()\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].apply(\n                lambda x: detect_and_convert_unit(x, feature)\n            )\n\n            # Count how many values were converted\n            changed = (original_values != df_clean[feature]).sum()\n            if changed > 0:\n                conversion_counts[feature] = changed\n\n    if conversion_counts:\n        print(f\"Converted values in {len(conversion_counts)} features:\")\n        for feature, count in sorted(conversion_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:10]:\n            print(f\"  {feature}: {count} values converted\")\n\n    # Step 4: Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].round(2)\n\n    # Step 5: Format all numeric values to ensure X.XX format\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Formatting numeric values to X.XX format...\")\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else x)\n\n    # mg/g\n    'Urine_Protein': {'min': 0.0, 'max': 30000.0, 'alt_factor': None},  # mg/dL\n    'Urine_pH': {'min': 4.0, 'max': 9.0, 'alt_factor': None},\n    'Urine_Specific_Gravity': {'min': 1.0, 'max': 1.1, 'alt_factor': None},\n\n    # Cardiac Markers\n    'BNP': {'min': 0.0, 'max': 10000.0, 'alt_factor': None},  # pg/mL\n    'NT_proBNP': {'min': 0.0, 'max': 50000.0, 'alt_factor': None},  # pg/mL\n    'Troponin_I': {'min': 0.0, 'max': 50000.0, 'alt_factor': None},  # ng/L (pg/mL in some labs)\n    'Troponin_T': {'min': 0.0, 'max': 10000.0, 'alt_factor': None},  # ng/L\n\n    # Thyroid\n    'Free_T4': {'min': 0.5, 'max': 100.0, 'alt_factor': 0.0129, 'alt_unit': 'pmol/L to ng/dL'},  # pmol/L -> ng/dL\n    'Free_T3': {'min': 1.0, 'max': 20.0, 'alt_factor': 0.154, 'alt_unit': 'pmol/L to pg/mL'},  # pmol/L -> pg/mL\n\n    # Blood Gas\n    'pH_Arterial': {'min': 6.5, 'max': 8.0, 'alt_factor': None},\n    'pCO2_Arterial': {'min': 5.0, 'max': 500.0, 'alt_factor': None},  # mmHg\n    'pO2_Arterial': {'min': 10.0, 'max': 600.0, 'alt_factor': None},  # mmHg\n    'Lactate': {'min': 0.1, 'max': 200.0, 'alt_factor': 0.111, 'alt_unit': 'mmol/L to mg/dL'},  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # Converting to string\n    2. Replacing comma with period (decimal separator)\n    3. Converting scientific notation to standard form\n    4. Converting to float\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Convert to string and strip whitespace\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    # Convert to float (handles scientific notation automatically)\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef detect_and_convert_unit(value, feature_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect if a value is using an alternative unit based on physiological range\n    and convert it to US conventional units if necessary.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or feature_name not in LAB_FEATURES_INFO:\n        return value\n\n    info = LAB_FEATURES_INFO[feature_name]\n    min_val = info['min']\n    max_val = info['max']\n    alt_factor = info.get('alt_factor')\n\n    # If the value is already within the expected range, no conversion needed\n    if min_val <= value <= max_val:\n        return value\n\n    # If there's no alternative unit conversion factor, keep as is\n    if alt_factor is None:\n        return value\n\n    # Try converting from alternative unit\n    converted_value = value * alt_factor\n\n    # If converted value falls within expected range, use it\n    if min_val <= converted_value <= max_val:\n        return converted_value\n\n    # If still outside range, return original (may be outlier)\n    return value\n\n\ndef harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize clinical lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading data from {input_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file)\n\n    print(f\"Initial dataset shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Initial row count: {len(df)}\")\n\n    # Step 1: Clean all numeric values (handle scientific notation and comma decimals)\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Cleaning numeric values (scientific notation, comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in df.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(clean_numeric_value)\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with missing values\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    initial_rows = len(df)\n    df_clean = df.dropna()\n    removed_rows = initial_rows - len(df_clean)\n    print(f\"Removed {removed_rows} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {len(df_clean)}\")\n\n    if len(df_clean) == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No complete records remaining after removing missing values!\")\n        return\n\n    # Step 3: Convert units to US conventional units\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Converting mixed units to US conventional units...\")\n    conversion_counts = {}\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature == 'patient_id':\n            continue\n\n        if feature in LAB_FEATURES_INFO:\n            # Apply unit detection and conversion\n            original_values = df_clean[feature].copy()\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].apply(\n                lambda x: detect_and_convert_unit(x, feature)\n            )\n\n            # Count how many values were converted\n            changed = (original_values != df_clean[feature]).sum()\n            if changed > 0:\n                conversion_counts[feature] = changed\n\n    if conversion_counts:\n        print(f\"Converted values in {len(conversion_counts)} features:\")\n        for feature, count in sorted(conversion_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:10]:\n            print(f\"  {feature}: {count} values converted\")\n\n    # Step 4: Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    df_clean_copy = df_clean.copy()\n    for col in df_clean_copy.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean_copy[col] = df_clean_copy[col].round(2)\n\n    # Step 5: Format all numeric values to ensure X.XX format\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Formatting numeric values to X.XX format...\")\n    for col in df_clean_copy.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean_copy[col] = df_clean_copy[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else x)\n\n    # Save harmonized data\n    print(f\"\\nSaving harmonized data to {output_file}...\")\n    df_clean_copy.to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n\n    print(f\"\\nHarmonization complete!\")\n    print(f\"Final dataset shape: {df_clean.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Output saved to: {output_file}\")\n\n    # Print some statistics\n    print(\"\\n=== Summary Statistics ===\")\n    print(f\"Input records: {initial_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Records with missing values removed: {removed_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Final complete records: {len(df_clean)}\")\n    print(f\"Data completeness: {len(df_clean)/initial_rows*100:.2f}%\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    input_file = \"/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv\"\n    output_file = \"/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv\"\n\n    harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":238,"oldLines":15,"newStart":238,"newLines":15,"lines":["     df_clean_copy.to_csv(output_file, index=False)"," ","     print(f\"\\nHarmonization complete!\")","-    print(f\"Final dataset shape: {df_clean.shape}\")","+    print(f\"Final dataset shape: {df_clean_copy.shape}\")","     print(f\"Output saved to: {output_file}\")"," ","     #
